> Statuses are NEVER marked PASS without evidence. A calculator only leaves
> NEEDS HUMAN REVIEW once a person has confirmed formula, units, signs,
> rounding, boundary/zero behavior and recorded it as a test case in
> `tests/unit/formula-qa-full.test.js` (the automated contract runner).
> Non-deterministic tools (random generators, UUIDs) are verified by
> structural PROPERTY QA (count/length/charset/range invariants).

Priority: deep-QA PASS first, then YMYL (finance/health) calculators, then the rest.
